# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - NATIVE CHEMICAL LAND RAIDS
# ============================================================================
# Overview:
# - Four exact-state ground delivery families: cylinder, projector, and
#   artillery have one variant per agent; armored delivery has matching light,
#   medium, and heavy chassis variants per agent.
# - Each raid starts at a supply node, assigns a qualifying land division,
#   prepares on its native timer, and reserves one exact chemical payload.
# - Air-delivered chemical raids remain in cbrn_chemical_air_raids.txt.
# - Static raid database aliases mirror the shared route costs and timings.
# ============================================================================

@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_DAYS = 5
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_DAYS = 7
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_DAYS = 14
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_DAYS = 10
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_COOLDOWN = 30
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_COOLDOWN = 45
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_COOLDOWN = 90
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_COOLDOWN = 60
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_CP = 8
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_CP = 12
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_CP = 16
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_CP = 15
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_PAYLOAD = 40
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_PAYLOAD = 70
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_PAYLOAD = 120
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_PAYLOAD = 80
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_MASKS = 4
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_DECON = 3
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_INSTRUMENTS = 2
@CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_SUPPORT = 6
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_MASKS = 6
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_DECON = 3
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_INSTRUMENTS = 5
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_SUPPORT = 6
@CBRN_LAND_PROJECTOR_CHASSIS = 1
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_MASKS = 9
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_DECON = 7
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_INSTRUMENTS = 8
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_SUPPORT = 12
# Mirrors cbrn_native_raid_reservation.artillery_total_shells. The route uses
# 120 attack shells plus one support shell from the same archetype.
@CBRN_LAND_ARTILLERY_TOTAL_SHELLS = 121
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_MASKS = 8
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_DECON = 6
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_INSTRUMENTS = 7
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_SUPPORT = 11
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_CHASSIS = 1
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_MOTORIZED = 5
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_FUEL_SUCCESS_REFERENCE = 0.50
@CBRN_LAND_ARMORED_FUEL_SUCCESS_BONUS = 0.05
@CBRN_LAND_AI_AGENT_SHARE = 0.11
@CBRN_LAND_AI_ARMORED_CLASS_SHARE = 0.333333
@CBRN_LAND_AI_MIN_SUCCESS = 0.35
@CBRN_LAND_SUCCESS_BASE = 0.50
@CBRN_LAND_CRITICAL_BASE = 0.12
@CBRN_LAND_DISASTER_BASE = 0.10
@CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_WEIGHT = 0.25
@CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_START = -0.20
@CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_REFERENCE = 1
@CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_WEIGHT = 0.10
@CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_START = -0.10
@CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_REFERENCE = 100
@CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_WEIGHT = 0.10
@CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_START = -0.10
@CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_REFERENCE = 1
@CBRN_LAND_RECON_WEIGHT = 0.05
@CBRN_LAND_RECON_REFERENCE = 10
@CBRN_LAND_INTEL_WEIGHT = 0.20
@CBRN_LAND_INTEL_REFERENCE = 100
@CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_ORG_DAMAGE = 0.65
@CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_STR_DAMAGE = 0.30
@CBRN_LAND_LIMITED_ORG_DAMAGE = 0.35
@CBRN_LAND_LIMITED_STR_DAMAGE = 0.10
@CBRN_LAND_SUCCESS_ORG_DAMAGE = 0.20
@CBRN_LAND_SUCCESS_STR_DAMAGE = 0.05
@CBRN_LAND_CRITICAL_ORG_DAMAGE = 0.10
@CBRN_LAND_CRITICAL_STR_DAMAGE = 0.02
@CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_XP = 0.05
@CBRN_LAND_LIMITED_XP = 0.15
@CBRN_LAND_SUCCESS_XP = 0.25
@CBRN_LAND_CRITICAL_XP = 0.35

types = {
	chemical_chlorine_cylinder_release_raid = {
		days_to_prepare = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_DAYS
		days_re_enable = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_COOLDOWN
		fire_only_once = no
		ai_min_success_chance = @CBRN_LAND_AI_MIN_SUCCESS
		category = chemical_raids
		custom_map_icon = GFX_decision_cbrn_battlefield_cylinder_release
		equipment_icon = GFX_chlorine_agent_lot_1_medium
		target_icon = GFX_other_target_icon
		target_loc_key = raid_target_name_chemical_state
		launch_sound = raid_land_launchable_sound
		command_power = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_CP
		unit_model = { entity = motorized_vehicle_entity }
		arrow = { type = land }
		ai_will_do = {
			base = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.cylinder_priority
			modifier = { factor = @CBRN_LAND_AI_AGENT_SHARE always = yes }
			modifier = { factor = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.disabled ROOT = { NOT = { cbrn_chemical_land_raid_ai_may_target_from = yes } } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.prepared_power ROOT = { cbrn_ai_has_battlefield_program_posture = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.retaliation ROOT = { cbrn_ai_has_retaliatory_arsenal_posture = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.first_use ROOT = { cbrn_ai_route_allows_first_use = yes } }
			modifier = { factor = constant:cbrn_battlefield_operation_ai.contaminated_state var:target_state = { cbrn_state_has_chemical_contamination = yes } }
		}
		visible = { has_tech = chlorine_gas  }
		show_target = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_target_tt
				hidden_trigger = { cbrn_chemical_land_raid_target_from_is_valid = yes var:target_state = { cbrn_battlefield_cylinder_target_state = yes } }
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_available_tt
				hidden_trigger = { cbrn_chemical_land_raid_cylinder_can_prepare = yes  cbrn_chemical_land_raid_target_from_is_valid = yes var:target_state = { cbrn_battlefield_cylinder_target_state = yes } }
			}
		}
		launchable = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_launch_tt
				hidden_trigger = { cbrn_chemical_land_raid_cylinder_can_launch = yes  cbrn_chemical_land_raid_target_from_is_valid = yes var:target_state = { cbrn_battlefield_cylinder_target_state = yes } }
			}
		}
		target_type = { state = { cbrn_battlefield_cylinder_target_state = yes } }
		starting_point = { building_types = { supply_node } }
		unit_requirements = { battalion_types = { infantry = { min = 3 } cbrn_gas_mask_decon_detachment = { min = 1 } } }
		unit_requirements = { battalion_types = { motorized = { min = 3 } cbrn_gas_mask_decon_detachment = { min = 1 } } }
		essential_equipment = { chlorine_agent_payload = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_PAYLOAD gas_mask_equipment = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_MASKS decontamination_equipment = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_DECON cbrn_instrument_equipment = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_INSTRUMENTS support_equipment = @CBRN_LAND_CYLINDER_SUPPORT }
		success_factors = {
			success = {
				base = @CBRN_LAND_SUCCESS_BASE
				experience = { weight = @CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_WEIGHT start_weight = @CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_START reference = @CBRN_LAND_EXPERIENCE_REFERENCE }
				organisation = { weight = @CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_WEIGHT start_weight = @CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_START reference = @CBRN_LAND_ORGANISATION_REFERENCE }
				strength = { weight = @CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_WEIGHT start_weight = @CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_START reference = @CBRN_LAND_STRENGTH_REFERENCE }
				recon = { weight = @CBRN_LAND_RECON_WEIGHT reference = @CBRN_LAND_RECON_REFERENCE }
				intel = { weight = @CBRN_LAND_INTEL_WEIGHT reference = @CBRN_LAND_INTEL_REFERENCE }
			}
			critical = { base = @CBRN_LAND_CRITICAL_BASE }
			disaster = { base = @CBRN_LAND_DISASTER_BASE }
		}
		success_levels = {
			failure = {
				actor_effects = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_failure_actor_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						var:actor_country = {
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_action_actor
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_route_cylinder_release
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_agent_chlorine
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_outcome_failure
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_phantom_zero
						}
						var:target_state = { save_event_target_as = cbrn_action_target_state }
						var:victim_country = { save_event_target_as = cbrn_action_victim_country }
						var:actor_country = { country_event = { id = cbrn_land_raid_bridge.1 } }
					}
					raid_damage_units = { org_damage = @CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_ORG_DAMAGE str_damage = @CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_STR_DAMAGE ratio = yes }
					raid_add_unit_experience = @CBRN_LAND_FAILURE_XP
					add_raid_history_entry = yes
				}
				victim_effects = { var:target_state = { custom_effect_tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_failure_target_tt } }
				visual_effect = { entity = "raid_failed_entity" animation = "attack" }
			}
			limited_success = {
				actor_effects = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_limited_actor_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						var:actor_country = {
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_action_actor
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_route_cylinder_release

							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_outcome_limited_success

					}

					raid_add_unit_experience = @CBRN_LAND_LIMITED_XP

				}

				visual_effect = { entity = "land_raids_limited_success_entity" animation = "attack" }
			}
			success = {
				actor_effects = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = raid_chemical_land_cylinder_success_actor_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						var:actor_country = {
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_action_actor
							save_event_target_as = cbrn_land_bridge_route_cylinder_release
